# Seed 81 on the AMD Ryzen 7 PRO 8840HS - nine hours, a gain at the fast end: the series goes on (Iteration 568)

`seed 81 9h then experiment review-checks.py spread then experiment appbench.py --zip ~/Downloads/appbench.zip --sample 16 then experiment carry-check.py --all` at 1531873ba. The chain: done. 32,906 designs, generations 0-128: 19,153 ok, 7,858 stopped after one round, 5,524 screened, 322 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run, 45 wrong output: apps, 4 died: kernel workload; 3,656 designs an hour.

**The front**: the true one 215 designs - the report's 215, the same.

**The fixed-box hypervolume**: true front 215 (215 inside the box), hypervolume 0.34746 | fastest 0.1387, smallest 14210, least memory 116 KB, fastest at 32 bits 0.1691. **The stopping rule** (stopping.py, results/stopping-series.md): verdict: seed 81 (at its last generation, 128 - the earlier runs there too) GAINS, by the hypervolume and the fastest - the hypervolume 0.34746 against 0.34106 (+1.9%, past its threshold); the fastest 0.1377 against 0.1418 (-2.9%, past its threshold); the smallest 14,210 against 14,051 (+1.1%); the least memory 112 KB against 108 KB (+4 KB); the fastest at 32 bits 0.1691 against 0.1702 (-0.7%) the count: 5 run(s) on the method; the earliest stop after seed 83, if every run until then gains nothing

**The extremes**: fastest 82f4ff7a02 0.1387, smallest f7bb332885 14,210 bytes, least memory 116 KB, fastest at 32 bits 52648fcf2f 0.1691.

**One round first**: 7,858 children stopped after one round, 28.8% of those measured; 829 audited in full, 0 of them on the front.

**The operators**: borrowed 38.4%, crossover 29.7%, mutation 31.9% of the children; the last fifth borrowed 39.1%, crossover 27.3%, mutation 33.6%; the front's entries per child 0.656% (215 of 32,762).

**The JIT designs**: 65 of the front, 21 of its fastest tenth's 21; jitmul 53, jitfuse 9, jitexec 2, jitos 0, jitrp 0.

**The fast end re-measured** (remeasure.json, 131 designs): the fastest ten 1.064 re-measured / in-run, the JIT designs 1.034, the rest 0.999.

**The experiments**: the spread at generation 126 - | build/evolve/db.jsonl | 126 | 18851 | 322 | 0.1387 | 14,210 | 112 | 1.0032e+06 | 0.6160 | 0.1691 |; appbench - **Spearman, in-run speed against the applications' geometric mean: 0.971 over 16 designs.**; the clock test - none; truefronts - none.

**The remote checks** (results/xcheck-seed81.md): dream.lan-prebuilt 24 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.891; dream.lan-prebuilt32 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.919; dream.lan-prebuilt64 30 designs measured on both: Spearman 0.928; rage.lan-native ?.

**Held out** (heldout.py): sieve (held out): 215 designs; Spearman with the selection speed 0.979 at the top, where selection chooses: the fastest third (71) 0.873, the fastest tenth (21) 0.723 regret: the selection's fastest, 82f4ff7a02, 0.073 held out; the held-out fastest, 477a582bf4, 0.062 - the pick +17.7%

**The curse** (curse.txt - rule 3, 0.98-1.05 works): fastest tenth    9 designs: median 1.060, range 1.020-1.088 fastest third   30 designs: median 1.045, range 0.945-1.088

**Progress**: the smallest binary + image (exact) gained in 22 generations; the last at generation 90 of 128; in the last third 2 gains, 16 bytes in all the best speed gained 2% or more in 14 generations; the last at generation 116 (27: 18.9%, 31: 7.7%, 35: 2.4%, 57: 2.4%, 116: 3.6%)

**The votes**: all four this run: scale, sharedcall, msc, opcall, jitdoes, jitcfa, jitrp, jitos, alignhot, jitvar, factor over the last 3 runs in the ledger (79, 80, 81): candidates - scale (12 of 12), sharedcall (12 of 12), msc (12 of 12), opcall (12 of 12), jitdoes (12 of 12), jitrp (12 of 12), jitos (12 of 12), alignhot (12 of 12); no keep vote and half the votes or more - none

**The rules** (rules.txt): 10 rules; 1 (The verdict) SEE, 3 (The fast gate) ATTENTION, 4 (The freeze) ATTENTION, 8 (tail) ATTENTION, 10 (Programs past 4 MB) SEE, the rest PASS

**The freezes lifted** (lifted.py): prediction 556 (the freezes were right; values as built): HOLDS - bytehdr, thinhdr, bss, lean off 1 on 5%- : yes; alignhot, msc, sharedcall each on under 10%: yes; doesfar and varcall off 1 nowhere: yes

**At 32 bits** (fail32.txt): at 32 bits: 20073 designs measured, 30 failed expected, no fault: 30 of 30 (cpt16's reach at 4-byte cells - Iteration 517); not a known cause: 0


**The deaths**: 45 wrong output: apps, 4 died: kernel workload, 322 died: reach limit at scale 0 - not run. deaths-check.py rebuilt them here (design, generation, in the run, genes, here): fb35526657 - 9 - wrong output: apps - jit+tracer - right - as s6. The control at 222430a (before the batch) - deaths-check: 0 of 1 rebuilt compute right here; 1 open.

**The chain on master**: process-checks.sh at 17566c6: build 0, run-tests PASS, validate 7 of 7 IDENTICAL, the gate alive 256 of 256, ans-check 0 of 56 differ (its negative control caught), deaths-check: 1 of 1 rebuilt compute right here; 0 open, the jail PASS.

**The verdict**: by the stopping rule (stopping.py) seed 81 **gains** at its last generation, 128 - the hypervolume
0.34746 against 0.34106 (+1.9%) and the fastest 0.1377 against 0.1418 (-2.9%), each past its threshold; the smallest
14,210, the series' largest there. At equal effort (the spread, generation 126) it is the fastest of the five runs on
the method at 64 and at 32 bits (0.1387, 0.1691 - the others 0.1418-0.1477, 0.1825-0.1885) and the largest at the small
end (14,210; the others 14,051-14,364). Not the fast gate's luck: its fastest tenth re-measures 1.060 of in-run, seeds
78-80's 1.061-1.094. Five runs on the method, the earliest stop after seed 83. **Predictions**: 566 fails - seed 81
gains, and its fastest at generation 126, 0.1387, is under 0.1389 (by 0.1%), unlike the four lifted runs before it;
its Tegra half holds - Spearman 0.971 between rage and the laptop on seed 77's 16 designs asked by id: the host is sound.

# Evolved VM designs

27382 designs evaluated, 19153 alive, 5524 screened - not measured (Iteration 200). Speed is the geometric mean of median cpu time / hand-made s6, long workloads (Iteration 100) over kernel, fib, corpus, loop, bubble, ackermann, apps,
relative to s6-cv8b; size is the self-hosting image, binary the stripped engine. sieve, parse is held out.
The front is over three objectives, equal (Iteration 75): speed, binary + image, peak memory - memory within 8 KB a tie (Iteration 77).


The Q-best design (binary + image x run time x memory - the knee; information): c610e092aa, speed 0.321, binary + image 21,789, memory 144 KB - Q 0.298 of s6-cv8b's.

## The front's genomes

Each design above, and any other the front was measured again with, as one JSON line: its record without the raw timings. A tool that takes a database (--db) reads this report as one - the lines that are not JSON are skipped - so results/ is all a new chat needs to rebuild a front design.
